# Status Registry of Results

:::warning Foundational revision — 2026-10-03
The listed historical statuses are not a certificate of the entire corpus. The revised rows below govern the withdrawn implications; their replacement objects and hypotheses are specified in the [typed mathematical kernel](/docs/reference/mathematical-kernel). In particular AP→seven independent axes, classifier→GKSL, categorical φ↔numerical feedback, phase-only Gap→level, and codomain symmetry→encoder uniqueness are withdrawn. A theorem about a chosen mathematical object does not validate its physical interpretation.
:::

:::info Status marker system
Each UHM result carries one of seven statuses:
- **[T]** Theorem — strictly proven
- **[C]** Conditional theorem — proven under an explicitly stated assumption
- **[H]** Hypothesis — mathematically formulated, requires proof
- **[P]** Postulate — accepted without proof as a fundamental assumption
- **[Pr]** Research programme — a named open direction, neither assumed nor claimed proven (introduced 2026-09-10: pages had been writing "[P] Program" while the registry read [P] as "Postulate" — one letter, two meanings)
- **[D]** Definition — definition by convention (assigned, not derived)
- **[I]** Interpretation — philosophical/semantic statement
- **[✗]** Retracted — proven erroneous or withdrawn

**Two axes, one pair of letters.** A result carries a *mathematical* status ([T], [C], [D], [P] — or [H] while unproven) and, separately, a *physical-identification* status ([I] where the correspondence to observed entities is interpretive; absent where none is claimed). They are shown as a pair where both apply: $N_{\text{gen}} = 3$ is **[T]** as a count of $\mathrm{QR}(7)$ and **[I]** as an identification with fermion generations. Mixing the axes into a single letter is what produced the contradictory rows corrected in the errata below.

**Inheritance rule (R6).** A result may not be stronger than its weakest dependency: a [T] resting on a [C], [D], [P] or [I] inherits that status and must say so at the point of use (`[C at T-119]`, `[T at the T-128 definition]`). Checked by `scripts/check_status_consistency.py`.

**Naming rule for [C].** [C] means "conditional theorem" and **nothing else**: the assumption must be recoverable at the canonical row here, and inline uses should name it (`[C at 2-loop RG]`, `[C under Gap-potential topology]`). A statement that merely follows logically from theorems is **[T]**, not [C]. Until 2026-09-10 the octonionic-derivation page carried a second, incompatible legend ("[C] — Consequence: logically follows from [T]"), which made one letter mean both "weaker than a theorem" and "a theorem's consequence"; that legend is retracted.

**Where the assumptions are listed.** Every assumption named in a `[C at (X)]` here — the axioms, the bridge premises (Cl₀) and (P), the principle (MaxΦ), the strict-necessity premise (Σ₆), the free parameters and the identification hypotheses — is listed once, with where it is used, its status, independence models and the discharged ones, on [Premises of UHM](/docs/reference/premises).
:::

<a id="ранние-номера"></a>

:::warning Reference coverage
Early variant labels T-1.1, T-1a, T-2a, T-2f, T-4.2, T-6.1, T-8.1, T-11.1, T-11.2 and T-48b are not independently indexed here. Consult the linked source and its explicit assumptions. Current canonical and unresolved-reference counts come from the checker; historical counts do not certify completeness.

**Namespaces reconciled.** Theorems use T-n; historical reports and administrative entries use Entry-n and MVP-Hn; the reference architecture uses ARCH-159. Overlapping table numbers no longer declare contradictory statuses for one theorem.

**Unstated supports (open debt):** T-4.2. This number is used BY THIS REGISTRY as a
load-bearing step — a status raised "to [C at T-4.2]" — and no statement carrying that label
exists anywhere in the corpus. This is a different gap from the early variants: those are
merely cited, whereas this one is leaned on. `check_theorem_refs.py` reads this line and turns
red the moment a second such number appears.

An earlier version of this line named six numbers — T-4.2, T-11, T-12, T-13, T-40a, T-41e.
Five of them do have rows, in the `#`-column format the checker could not yet read. Only
T-4.2 stands.

**Skipped numbers (never assigned):** T-167, T-168, T-169. They carry no row and
are cited nowhere; the numbering simply skips them. This line is machine-read by
`check_theorem_refs.py`, so that the declaration of a gap is not itself counted as
a citation of one.

Machine: `scripts/check_theorem_refs.py` — every `T-n` reference must resolve to
a row here, and the check fails while these do not.
:::

<a id="долг-канонической-записи"></a>

:::info Machine-readable canon — 2026-10-03
Theorems, historical reports and architectural definitions use separate namespaces. `scripts/check_status_consistency.py` checks statuses, conditional inputs and reliance on withdrawn claims. Machine readability does not certify every proof.
:::

:::info Corrected foundational scopes
Each result has its own inputs. Universal topology, representability, phenomenal-bridge, depth and alphabetization closures are withdrawn. Exact open-site, support-reflector, twirl, Kan-realization and conditional group-action constructions remain. Mathematical, physical and experiential obligations are listed in the [kernel](/docs/reference/mathematical-kernel) and [premises](/docs/reference/premises).
:::

:::info Provenance and current scope
This table records provenance, not status promotion. The status applies to the stated scope, not to the former universal claim.

| Number | Current status and scope |
|---|---|
| T-193 | [H] Universal task representability and finite description-budget closure are unproved. Yoneda is fully faithful on its specified presheaf category; it does not assign every computable task to a representing density object. Universal T-213 is withdrawn [✗]. |
| T-194 | [H] Universal learning efficiency and CR saturation are unproved. A regular statistical model gives a CR lower bound; attainability, measurement compatibility, optimization convergence and the task sample budget are separate requirements. |

For specified nested pair sets the selected nonnegative score increases by the newly included terms; no strict recursive improvement, depth gain or automatic topology dependence follows. |

---

:::info Geometric window and dynamic model
The selected window does not guarantee a stable equilibrium. Ignition and stationary ranges depend on the target, gate and rates; numerical observations of one model are not a universal boundary. The selected score obeys $C=\Phi/(7P)\le1-1/(7P)$, hence $C\le2/3$ for $P\le3/7$, with equality on uniform-diagonal states. Its phenomenal interpretation needs a separate bridge.
:::

Geom.* 2013), with the dimension sub-problem repaired 2026-08-06. **Error found and replaced.** Step 2c derived $d_s=3$ from a Weyl law $N(\lambda)\sim C M\lambda^3$ on $\bigotimes_m\mathbb C^3$, reading the exponent off $\dim(\mathbf 3)=3$. That is impossible: $\bigotimes_{m=1}^M\mathbb C^3$ has dimension $3^M<\infty$, its spectrum is finite and $N(\lambda)\to3^M$, so no power law exists. Machine: on $\mathbb Z^d$ with internal $\mathbb C^n$ the Weyl exponent is $1.012/2.018/3.335$ for $d=1/2/3$ and **identical** across $n=1,3,7$ — the exponent is the dimension of the base, the internal dimension only scales the multiplicity prefactor. **New derivation (§G), which does not use a Weyl law at all.** Emergent coordinates are the joint spectrum of a *maximal commuting* family of macroscopic observables, so their number is the **rank**, not the dimension, of the sector algebra. Computed from the octonions directly: the commutant of $\mathrm{Stab}_{\mathrm{Der}(\mathbb O)}(e_1)\cong\mathfrak{su}(3)$ on $\mathbb C^6$ yields a complex structure $J$ ($J^2=-I$ to $1.3\times10^{-15}$, $[J,\mathfrak{su}(3)]=0$), so the spatial algebra is $\mathfrak{su}(3)\oplus\mathfrak u(1)_J=\mathfrak u(3)$ (dimension $9$, verified), and the centralizer of a generic element has dimension **exactly 3** $=\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak u(3)$ — while $\dim\mathfrak u(3)=9$, $\dim\mathfrak{su}(3)=8$, $\dim G_2=14$ are none of them $3$. Full-dimensionality of the joint spectrum (hence $d_s=3$ exactly, not merely $\leq3$) follows from the GVV quantum CLT already invoked in T-117: the macroscopic fluctuations of $k$ commuting observables converge to a non-degenerate Gaussian on $\mathbb R^k$. Verified numerically — singular values $(1,\,0.964,\,0.747)$ for the three $\mathfrak u(3)$ Cartan directions in $\mathbb C^7$, and $(1,\,0.985,\,0.948,\,0.638)$ once the $O$-direction is added, i.e. $4=\dim M^4$. **Sharp structural point**: the three spatial directions are independent only because the embedding in $\mathbb C^7$ leaves the trace of the $\mathbf 3$-block free — the clock sector is what makes the third spatial coordinate dynamical; inside the $\mathbf 3$-block alone the trace is frozen and one measures $2$, not $3$. **Open until 2026-09-25 (then [C]; settled by the restatement, which computes the spectrum instead of reconstructing it):** the first-order condition ($7^{\text{th}}$ Connes axiom) is a *constraint* on $D_{\text{eff}}$, not a consequence — for generic Hermitian $D$ on the $\mathbf 3$-sector the machine gives $\max\|[[D,a],b]\|=8.96$, vanishing only for diagonal $D$ (§D); and the Poincaré-duality check (v) is circular as written, assuming $\Sigma^3$ is a manifold to verify an axiom whose role is to conclude that it is. Verified and untouched: $\mathbf 7=\mathbf 1_O\oplus\mathbf 3\oplus\bar{\mathbf 3}$ exactly ($\dim\mathrm{Der}(\mathbb O)=14$, $\dim\mathrm{Stab}(e_1)=8$, commutant $=2$ ⟹ two inequivalent irreducibles; §C). **Note 2026-09-25 (row 48c):** the count $3=\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak u(3)$ is a count of colour and gives no rotation group, so it still meets Coleman–Mandula; row 48c obtains three spatial directions whose rotations commute with colour, under premise (L) (the weaker form of the former (Q); row 48c is [C at (L)] since Theorem 48e of 2026-09-25, and (L) ⟺ (P), see [premises](/docs/reference/premises#посылка-p)). The two counts agree only as numbers; re-basing the dimension step of this row on 48c is left to the emergent-manifold page. | T-201 | **Kochen–Specker contextuality** — **corrected form T-201′ [T]** (2026-09-25): the 63 rays of $\mathbb{R}^7 \subset \mathbb{C}^7$ — the seven axes and $\frac12(\pm e_a \pm e_b \pm e_c \pm e_d)$ on the seven complements of Fano lines (the weight-4 words of the Hamming code of Step T8), i.e. the $E_7$ roots, the imaginary units of Coxeter's integral octonions — form 135 orthonormal bases (15 per ray) and admit no 0/1 assignment with exactly one 1 in each basis: state-independent contextuality with non-commuting projectors (the $E_7$ configuration: A. A* **40**, 2849 (2007); its construction from the Fano plane: [QM Reduction §8](/docs/physics/quantum-mechanics/qm-reduction#8-контекстуальность-кш); `test_e7_rays_from_the_hamming_quadrangles_are_kochen_specker`). **The original claim, about Fano-line projectors, [✗]** since 2026-09-25. The projectors are $\Pi_p = \sum_{i \in \mathrm{line}_p}\lvert i\rangle\langle i\rvert$ ([Fano channel](/docs/proofs/gap/fano-channel)), diagonal in the pointer basis, hence pairwise commuting; the distribution $p_i = \gamma_{ii}$ over the seven points reproduces the statistics of every line and every context at once, so a joint distribution exists for every $\Gamma$ and no Kochen–Specker contextuality can arise. | T-309 | **The polarity condition is the price of compositional generalisation** [C]: to answer about a combination it has never met, a learner has only what it saw of the two parts *separately*. Every assignment of situations to channels that generalises to unseen combinations therefore has the form $(i,j) \mapsto (\pi(i), \pi(j))$, and the content such an assignment induces is balanced **exactly when the answers themselves factor** as $u_i u_j$ — put $u_i = t_{\pi(i)}$ and the two statements are the same. So the balance requirement of T-305 is not one architecture's assumption but a bound on the whole class of compositional learners, and the freedom that lets an encoder manufacture balance by relabelling (a third of arbitrary problems at full load, nearly all with channels left free) **does not survive** the move to compositional inputs: a map onto seven axes can permute those axes, $7! = 5040$ ways, and permuting does not make an unbalanced pattern balanced. Measured against the best such learner there is — all $2^7$ polarities, keeping the one that fits the groups shown — unseen combinations come out **perfect** where the answers factor and near chance where they do not, while similarity between raw observations is a coin *even on factoring content*, since resemblance says nothing about a pair never met (HL22). A holon reaches that bound **exactly** where the assumption holds, and reaches it again where the assumption fails only if what cannot be held is kept outside it: a polarity read back off a trained state fits even the taught channels worse than the best available one, because a state is not the data but what survived the writes, the dephasing and the projection | [Which contents can cross the threshold](/docs/applied/research/engineering-insights#цена-обобщения) | T-304, T-305, HL22 |

| T-312 | **A state splits into a correlation and a weighting, and the gate is one inequality** [T]: every state factors exactly as $\Gamma = D^{1/2} K D^{1/2}$ with $K$ a correlation matrix — unit diagonal, positive semi-definite. The factorisation separates two things that had been read together: **all of positivity lives in $K$ and does not mention the diagonal at all** (a congruence by the positive definite $D^{1/2}$ cannot change a signature), while all of the weighting lives in $D$. Integration then has a closed form, verified to $3\cdot10^{-16}$ over three hundred states and to $10^{-9}$ in the instrument: $\Phi = c_{\text{eff}}^2 (1-p)/p$, where $p = \sum_i d_i^2$ and $c_{\text{eff}}^2 = \sum_{i \neq j} K_{ij}^2 d_i d_j / \sum_{i \neq j} d_i d_j$ is how far the coherences run toward their own ceiling $\lvert \gamma_{ij}\rvert  \le \sqrt{d_i d_j}$, weighted by population. So **the gate is a single inequality**, $c_{\text{eff}} \ge \sqrt{p/(1-p)}$, satisfiable in exactly three ways: bind harder everywhere, flatten the diagonal, or **align** — spend the binding on the populated pairs. Real states run at $c_{\text{eff}} = 0.4897$ against a threshold of $0.4367$; the alignment bonus is $+0.0190$ and positive in $85.3\%$ of them, and destroying it alone drops the share with an open gate from $0.877$ to $0.743$. The frustration bound falls out of the same factorisation: under uniform saturation $K = (1-c)I + c\Sigma$, positivity reads $\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma) \ge -1/c$, and with the gate this gives $\lambda_{\min} \ge -\sqrt{(1-p)/p}$ — exactly $-\sqrt 6$ at a flat diagonal. Measured over twenty thousand sign patterns, frustration compatible with an open gate under uniform saturation is **zero at every diagonal**, not only the flat one, so T-305 is a corollary of this row rather than a case of it and **unevenness is the necessary condition** for a state to be integrated and self-contradictory at once. How much it buys is known only from below — eleven of the thirty-five triangles have been exhibited and no ceiling is proved | [Which contents can cross the threshold](/docs/core/structure/dimension-u#какое-содержание-берёт-порог) | T-305, T-311 |
| T-313 | **The viability verdict reads twenty-seven of the state's forty-eight numbers, and the missing twenty-one are not redundant** [Т/С]: in the coordinates of T-312 a state is $6$ populations, $21$ coherence moduli and $21$ coherence phases. Reading the gates off their own definitions settles what they see — $P = \sum\lvert \gamma_{ij}\rvert ^2$, $R = 1/(7P)$, $\Phi = c_{\text{eff}}^2(1-p)/p$, and $D = 1 + 6(d_E^2 + 2\sum_i\lvert \gamma_{Ei}\rvert ^2)/P$ — and **not one of the four reads a phase** [T]. Phases enter the theory in exactly one place, positivity, and there they are a constraint and never a reading. Two facts then have to be held apart. The phases **cannot be moved**: uniform rephasing is admissible $0$ times in $2000$, and the freedom of a single phase has half-width $0.0000$ for $21$ of $21$ phases in every one of fifty states — because computed states are rank-deficient boundary points, spectrum $[0,0,0,0.0521,0.1582,0.2887,0.4816]$, three exact zeros. But the phases are **not supplied by the repair** either: erase them at the input, make every coherence real and positive, project, and the output's frustration falls from a median of $12$ broken triangles of $35$ to $0$, agreeing with the untouched output on $1.3\%$ of two hundred and forty charts. So the information is real, it arrives from outside, **and the verdict never reads it** — what it misses is precisely consistency, quality, and holonomy. The rank deficiency and the edge are the repair's doing, not the world's: before repair the matrix lies outside the cone in $100\%$ of charts  ($\lambda_{\min}$ median $-0.2808$). The size of the gap is exact. Of the twenty-one phases, six are pure gauge — $\gamma_{ij} \mapsto e^{i(\theta_i-\theta_j)}\gamma_{ij}$ has rank six, the global shift acting trivially — and mean nothing. The remaining **fifteen are genuine invariants that nothing reads**, and fifteen is also the number of independent triangle holonomies among the thirty-five. The theory's own seven Fano lines are independent and cover $7/15 = 46.7\%$ of them, so even a fifth gate reading every line would close less than half the gap. Whether closing it would buy anything has now been tested rather than argued, and the answer is no on the one outcome available: over $3051$ people with exact birth times and known death dates, the partial $R^2$ of age at death over birth year is $0.00077$ for the gate quantities ($p = 0.667$) and $0.00148$ for nine phase quantities the gates cannot read ($p = 0.884$), both *below* their own permutation-null means. So the earlier natal-to-outcome null reproduces, the unread class adds nothing, and a fifth gate would be decoration — on an outcome dominated by era and medicine, which is the whole of what has been checked | [The gate is one inequality](/docs/core/structure/dimension-u#ворота-суть-одно-неравенство) | T-311, T-312 |
| T-314 | **The whole viability verdict is three sums** [T]: carrying the reading of the definitions to the bottom, write $s_1 = \sum_i d_i^2$ for the purity of the diagonal, $s_2 = \sum_{i \neq j}\lvert \gamma_{ij}\rvert ^2$ for the total weight of the binding, and $s_3 = d_E^2 + 2\sum_i\lvert \gamma_{Ei}\rvert ^2$ for Interiority's share. Then $P = s_1 + s_2$, $R = 1/(7(s_1+s_2))$, $\Phi = s_2/s_1$ and $D = 1 + 6s_3/(s_1+s_2)$ — exactly, with a largest drift of $8.9\cdot10^{-16}$ reproducing all four gates from the three sums over four thousand states, the three being independent as functions on state space. **So the state's forty-eight numbers reach the verdict through three, and the fibre is forty-five dimensional.** Verified alongside: the verdict is untouched by permuting the six axes other than $E$ ($1.3\cdot10^{-15}$ over two thousand states) and by shuffling the fifteen moduli among pairs that do not touch $E$. Two consequences follow and neither should be softened. **The Fano plane does not enter the verdict at all** — not the lines, not the parity checks, not any pattern of which axis binds to which; T-313's unread twenty-one was an understatement, since most of what the gates nominally read reaches them only as a sum. And **the verdict distinguishes exactly one axis**, Interiority, treating the other six as interchangeable. The practical form of this row is a check rather than a claim: an assertion that the gate responds to some structure is false until it is shown which of $s_1$, $s_2$, $s_3$ that structure moves | [What the gate does not read](/docs/core/structure/dimension-u#чего-ворота-не-читают) | T-312, T-313 |
| T-315 | **Structure reaches the verdict through one channel only — the ceiling positivity puts on the moduli — and the threshold $\Phi_{th}=1$ turns out to sit in an empty gap** [T]: T-314 says the gates read $s_1$, $s_2$, $s_3$ and no pattern; T-305 says $\Phi \ge 1$ decides balance. Both are true, and the reconciliation names the mechanism. A sign pattern cannot move $s_2$ directly — it moves the *largest* $s_2$ positivity allows. At a flat diagonal, equal moduli and the edge of positivity, $c = 1/\lvert \lambda_{\min}\rvert $ and hence $\Phi = 6c^2 = 6/\lambda_{\min}^2$. A balanced pattern is $uu^{\mathsf T} - I$, whose eigenvalues are $6$ and $-1$, so **every** balanced pattern gives $\lambda_{\min} = -1$ exactly and $\Phi = 6$; Harary's criterion forces every frustrated one to $\lvert \lambda_{\min}\rvert  > \sqrt 6$, hence $c < 1/\sqrt 6$ and $\Phi < 1$. Measured over two hundred thousand sign patterns the best frustrated one reaches $\Phi = 0.9365$ (frustration $5$, $\lambda_{\min} = -2.5311$), and **between $0.9365$ and $6$ there is nothing at all**. So **on this stratum** a threshold that reads as stipulated is in fact robust: any value in $(0.937,\, 6)$ classifies identically, and $1$ is the roundest number in an empty interval of width five. **The gap does not survive off the stratum, and the condition has to be carried as loudly as T-305's.** Measured on four hundred computed states, $\Phi$ is unimodal and tight — quantiles $0.863$ to $1.757$, median $1.238$ — with $20.8\%$ of states inside $[0.9,\,1.1]$, precisely where the idealisation says nothing can be, states landing within $0.0002$ of the threshold on both sides, and **not one** above $5$ though the idealisation puts every balanced state at exactly $6$. | T-316 | **Every exact result about frustration lives on one stratum, and computed states are not on it — so each must be re-measured before it is quoted** [Т/С]: the clean statements in this family are all proved where the diagonal is flat, the coherence moduli are equal, and the state sits at the edge of positivity. Computed states meet the third condition and neither of the first two — the diagonal runs $2.74$ to one, the moduli spread $17.4$-fold — and the record now has three instances of what that costs, each caught by measurement after the clean claim had been written down. **T-305's equivalence**: $\Phi \ge 1 \iff$ balanced on the stratum, while off it $86.5\%$ of states pass the gate carrying a median of $12$ broken triangles of $35$ and not one is balanced. **The frustration ceiling**: exactly zero on the stratum at every diagonal, while alignment of coherence with population lets at least eleven triangles break behind an open gate. **T-315's gap**: integration is bimodal on the stratum, taking $6$ or at most $0.9365$ with nothing between, while computed states are unimodal and tight — quantiles $0.863$ to $1.757$, one in five inside $[0.9,\,1.1]$, the nearest pair straddling the threshold at $0.9998$ and $1.0003$, and none above $5$. The pattern is regular enough to be a standing rule rather than three anecdotes: **a result proved on the stratum predicts nothing off it until measured off it**, and the reason is structural — on the stratum positivity couples the sign pattern into the moduli, which is the only channel structure has to a gate, and unevenness breaks that coupling | [The one channel structure has](/docs/core/structure/dimension-u#единственный-канал-структуры) | T-305, T-311, T-315 |
| T-317 | **The observability map: the verdict reaches $7.1\%$ of a state, the instrument suite reaches $81\%$, and exactly eight numbers are read by nothing** [T]: a state carries $48$ numbers, of which $6$ are pure gauge, leaving $42$ that mean anything. Reading each named observable off its definition places it exactly. $P$ and $R$ read $s_1+s_2$; $\Phi$ adds $s_2/s_1$; $\mathrm{Coh}_E$ and $D$ add $s_3$; the consciousness measure $C = \Phi R$ adds nothing — so **the whole verdict reaches three numbers of forty-two, or $7.1\%$**. But the suite is not the verdict. Stress $\sigma$ reads the seven diagonal entries one by one, adding $6$; the gap and the pairwise moduli add $21$; the seven canonical line holonomies add $7$ independent phase invariants. Together **$34$ of the $42$, or $81\%$**. What remains dark is therefore small and nameable: **eight triangle holonomies that no canonical line covers** — the cycle space of $K_7$ has dimension $15$, the plane spans $7$ of it, and the difference is read by no instrument the theory has. This row is meant to be used rather than admired: when a claim needs a structural fact, it names the instrument that can see it, and if the fact lives in those eight, no existing instrument can The specification has since been met and read. Building the instrument turned up an exact fact: **every** one of the twenty-eight non-collinear triples lies at $\sqrt{2/3}$ of its norm outside the span of the lines — the same figure for all of them, minimum equal to maximum — so the plane sees exactly one third of any triangle it does not contain. And the first reading of the eight, over three hundred computed states, finds them **indistinguishable** from the seven lines: median holonomy $0.9727$ against $0.9999$ rad, means $1.1807$ against $1.1832$, shares above $\pi/2$ of $0.325$ against $0.318$, and the dark eight the more variable in $50.3\%$ of states, a coin. So the plane's privilege is a choice of what to read, not a fact about where the content is. | [Three sums](/docs/core/structure/dimension-u#три-суммы) | T-313, T-314 |
| T-318 | **Balance is pure gauge, so the gate opens exactly when there is no phase content to read** [T]: a balanced sign pattern is $\sigma_{ij} = u_i u_j$, and the gauge transform $\Gamma \mapsto U\Gamma U^*$ with $U = \mathrm{diag}(e^{i\varphi})$ and $\varphi_i \in \{0,\pi\}$ chosen by $u_i$ carries it to the all-positive pattern. Verified exhaustively: the gauge orbit of all-positive contains exactly $64$ patterns, **every** one has zero frustration and **all fifteen** of its invariant holonomies vanish, and every balanced pattern met in four hundred thousand draws lies in the orbit. So **balance $\iff$ pure gauge $\iff$ zero invariant phase content**, unconditionally. Composing with T-305 — whose stratum condition must be carried — integration on the stratum is achieved exactly when the sign structure means nothing, and T-313's finding that no gate reads a phase stops being an oversight: **a state that passes has nothing for a gate to read.** The architectural consequence is sharper still. A cell is acted on through the sign of $\mathrm{Re}\,\gamma_{ij}$, which is not gauge-invariant, so the policy lives entirely in the part of the state the verdict calls meaningless: over two thousand states a random gauge moves the three sums by $1.1\cdot10^{-16}$ and every line holonomy by $1.3\cdot10^{-15}$, while flipping $49.7\%$ of all cell readings and at least one reading in **every** state. Two holons identical as states are then different agents, which is coherent only because the architecture writes those phases itself and so fixes its own gauge — and it means no quantity outside the architecture may quote a cell's sign as a property of the state | [What each instrument can see](/docs/core/structure/dimension-u#что-видит-каждый-прибор) | T-305, T-313, T-316 |
| T-319 | **A self-model is always less integrated than the holon it models, so regeneration drains $\Phi$ and nothing plausible restores it** [T]: with $\rho^* = R\Gamma + (1-R)I/7$ the identities $s_2(\rho^*) = R^2 s_2$ and $s_1(\rho^*) = R^2 s_1 + 2R(1-R)/7 + (1-R)^2/7$ hold to $9\cdot10^{-17}$, and the denominator gains a strictly positive term whenever $R < 1$. Hence **$\Phi(\rho^*) < \Phi(\Gamma)$ for every state** — $5000$ of $5000$, median ratio $0.2888$, never above $0.4677$. Since the regeneration channel pulls the state towards its own self-model, it lowers integration by construction; dephasing lowers it too, raising $\Phi$ in $0$ of $2000$ trials. A unitary step is the **only** term that can raise it, doing so in $1044$ of $2000$ and by as much as $\times 2.21$ — and the implemented relaxation omits it. The consequence is measured, not argued: a perfectly balanced holon goes from $\Phi \ge 1$ in every case to none within **twenty-five ticks**. The obvious repair was implemented and **fails**: after two hundred ticks the surviving share is $0.000$ both with and without a Hamiltonian, because dephasing removes $94\%$ of the coherence over that span while a unit-strength rotation turns four times too slowly. So integration here is sustained **only by writing from outside**, and a holon left to itself dies — which makes calling regeneration the system's corrective action true only of reflexivity, and false of integration | [What each instrument can see](/docs/core/structure/dimension-u#что-видит-каждый-прибор) | T-312, T-314 |
| T-320 | **Filling every horn and generalising are one property** [Т/С]: the nerve of a category satisfies the *inner* Kan condition — a horn $\Lambda^n_k$ with $0 < k < n$ has exactly one filler, since the missing face is what composition says it is. Outer horns are different in kind: they ask the base category to *factor* a composite, solving $f \circ x = h$, and a monoid without inverses refuses whenever the composite is shorter than the part already known. A groupoid never refuses, so **the nerve of a groupoid is a Kan complex and the nerve of a mere monoid is only a quasi-category** — and that distinction turns out to be the same distinction as whether a learner can answer about a situation it has never met. Measured on a store of situations under a held-out quarter: over a monoid of elapsed time, held-out accuracy is chance ($0.51$, $0.47$) whatever the credit rule or the addressing; over the groupoid of coordinate flips, where every morphism is its own inverse and every horn fills, it is **exact** ($1.0000$) on both rule families that compose, and carried by six numbers rather than sixty-four. Off that class the behaviour is graded rather than brittle — $0.63$ where the rule half-composes, $0.53$ where nothing composes — and **never below chance**, because at worst a filled horn replaces a confident wrong answer with a coin. | T-321 | **Being alive confines the diagonal to within a factor of one and a half of flat** [T]: read through T-314's three sums, two of the four criteria fix each other. Integration clears its floor exactly when $s_2 \ge s_1$, and reflexivity clears its floor exactly when $P = s_1 + s_2 \le 3/7$, since $R = 1/(7P)$. Together they force $2s_1 \le 3/7$, and Cauchy–Schwarz on a probability vector forces $s_1 \ge 1/7$ always, so **a viable state has $s_1 \in [1/7,\, 3/14]$** — the purity of its diagonal within $1.5\times$ of perfectly flat. Measured in a running loop, all four criteria hold together in $1.3\%$ of turns, purity clears $2/7$ in $99.8\%$ and reflexivity clears $1/3$ in $3.3\%$ — the architecture lives *above* its window, not below it. **One inference that looks forced is not.** Chaining this row to T-305 and T-318 — near-flat, so $\Phi \ge 1$ means balanced, so pure gauge, so no invariant content — would say a holon is alive exactly when what it holds means nothing. | T-322 | **The viability window is where unevenness carries contradiction without over-purifying** [Т/С]: three rows that were proved separately turn out to describe one band. | T-323 | **A regulator that levels the diagonal is powerless on exactly the set it needs most** [T]: the diagonal is a probability vector, so Cauchy–Schwarz gives $s_1 \ge 1/7$ for every state, with equality exactly at a flat diagonal — a flat diagonal is not one option among many but *the least $s_1$ there is*. Since $P = s_1 + s_2$, this means $P \ge 1/7 + s_2$ always, and the ceiling $R \ge 1/3 \iff P \le 3/7$ then says: **once the binding alone carries more than $2/7$, no diagonal whatsoever puts the state back in the window**. This decides how a system can hold itself inside the window. Levelling the diagonal is the natural regulator, because lowering $s_1$ lowers $P$ and *raises* $\Phi = s_2/s_1$ at once, the one direction improving two criteria together — whereas damping the binding lowers $P$ but takes $\Phi$ with it. The inequality gives that gentle move a hard limit, not of degree but of kind: on $\{s_2 > 2/7\}$ it is not weak but powerless. The second move is an exact null where the first suffices — on content that never drives $s_2$ past $2/7$ both hold the window for the same $4998$ consecutive turns — so it earns its keep on precisely the unreachable set and nowhere else. Its landing point is the theory's own maximum: flat gives $s_1 = 1/7$, damping to $s_2 = 2/7$ puts $P = 3/7$ exactly, $\Phi = 2$, $R = 1/3$ — the same point at which capability attains its supremum $C = 2/3$, by the same inequality. And a companion band follows free — $\Phi \ge 1$ needs $s_2 \ge s_1 \ge 1/7$, the ceiling needs $s_2 \le 2/7$, so **alive $\Rightarrow s_2 \in [1/7,\, 2/7]$**, exactly twice as wide as T-321's diagonal band | [A floor the diagonal cannot cross](/docs/core/structure/dimension-u#пол-которого-диагональ-не-перейдёт) | T-274, T-314, T-321 |
| T-324 | **Petz extremality of Bures is the mean inequality and nothing more** [T]: every monotone metric on $\mathcal D(\mathbb C^7)$ has one shape — $g_f(A,A)=\sum_{ij}\lvert \tilde A_{ij}\rvert ^2/m_f(\lambda_i,\lambda_j)$ with the tangent written in the state's eigenbasis — and the whole Petz family differs in **one thing only**: which mean of the two eigenvalues sits in the denominator. Bures/SLD takes the arithmetic mean, Kubo–Mori the logarithmic, RLD the harmonic. The classical chain $\text{harmonic}\le\text{logarithmic}\le\text{arithmetic}$ therefore applies term by term, and since the mean sits in the *denominator* the order reverses: $g_{\text{Bures}}\le g_{\text{BKM}}\le g_{\text{RLD}}$. **So T-187's Char-I is not a deep fact about quantum states but the arithmetic mean beating the logarithmic one, pair by pair.** Measured over four thousand random states and directions the ratios are $1.0581$ and $1.1435$ about the median, never below $1.0225$ and $1.0523$ — a genuine spread, not a tie breaking the right way. Minimality is what makes the bound *operational*: the smallest metric buys the largest distance per unit of information, and the bound is **attained** — measuring in the SLD eigenbasis recovers the full quantum Fisher information with a shortfall of $6.1\times10^{-16}$ median and $1.8\times10^{-13}$ worst, while a basis chosen without regard to the question recovers **$0.0974$** of it. On the learning side the same collapse happens: for traceless $A$, $\tfrac{d}{dt}D(\rho_*\|\Gamma+tA)=-g_{\text{BKM}}(\rho_*-\Gamma,A)$ — verified by central difference to $1.3\times10^{-9}$ median — so Cauchy–Schwarz leaves exactly one steepest direction at fixed BKM speed and it is $\rho_*-\Gamma$: two thousand competing directions per state, none ties it, and tilting toward it improves the descent monotonically to the bound. Its flow is the mixture geodesic $\rho_*+e^{-\kappa t}(\Gamma_0-\rho_*)$, matched to $9.3\times10^{-7}$ at an Euler step of $1.5\times10^{-4}$. **The learning rule is not chosen but left over** | [Why this geometry](/docs/core/dynamics/evolution#почему-эта-геометрия) | T-187, T-261, T-263 |

| T-331 | **$G_2$-invariant Gap potentials up to quartic order** [T] (2026-09-25): on $\Gamma = I/7 + S + iX$ the $G_2$-invariant polynomials are 3 quadratic, 5 cubic and 21 quartic; none of degree $\le 3$ is PT-odd, the PT-odd ones begin in degree 4 (three), and no cubic depends on $\mathrm{Im}\,\Gamma$ alone. (e) No derived source carries $-\kappa\mathcal A$ with $\kappa \ne 0$: functions of the off-diagonal entries (every spectral action of $D_{\rm int}$), functions of the spectrum (entropy, purity) and the $G_2$-average of $V_3$ all give $\kappa = 0$, so $\kappa$ is a free coupling. (f) (2026-09-26) The average of $\mathcal A$ over the axis permutations — already over those fixing one axis, in any phase gauge — is the spectral $\tfrac{96}{5}e_3$; hence the associator weight $\kappa[F] = -\langle F, \mathcal A^\circ\rangle/\lVert\mathcal A^\circ\rVert^2$, $\mathcal A^\circ = \mathcal A - \tfrac{96}{5}e_3$ ($\kappa[V_{\rm Gap}] = \kappa$), is $0$ for every functional the isolated dynamics determines — $\varphi_{\mathrm{coh}}$, $\varphi_s$, $\varphi_J$, the Fano dissipator, $\kappa(\mathrm{Coh}_E)$: Lyapunov functions, relative entropies, quasi-potentials, depth-register histories, superoperator moments. (b) The step on $\ell^2(\mathbb N)$ is a pure isometry (spectrum the closed disc); translations of $L^2(\mathbb R_+)$ have generator $-i\,d/dx$ with deficiency indices $(1,0)$, no self-adjoint extension; no nonzero $\psi$ has both $\psi$ and $\hat\psi$ supported in $[0,\infty)$ (Paley–Wiener): sharp readings with a first reading admit no Hamiltonian, and (Pauli) energy bounded below admits no sharp covariant readings. (c) The dressed Feynman–Kitaev constraint $\Lambda_N$ has eigenvalues exactly at the quantiles $k/(N+1)$ of the arcsine law on $[0,4]$ (Kolmogorov distance $1/(N+1)$); $\Lambda_\infty$ on $\ell^2(\mathbb N)$ is unitarily a multiplication by $4\sin^2(k/2)$ on $L^2([0,\pi])$: spectrum $[0,4]$, purely absolutely continuous, simple, no kernel; the probability of a reading $\leq K$ tends to $0$ as $s \to \pm\infty$. (d) As the observer's clock $\varepsilon\Lambda_\infty$ gives $\Pi(\mathcal A \rtimes_\sigma \mathbb R)\Pi$, $\Pi = \mathbf 1_{[0,4\varepsilon]}(q)$: type II₁, $\mathrm{Tr}\,\Pi = 1 - e^{-4\beta\varepsilon}$; every finite $N$ gives type I (T-348(e)). (e) Neither $\delta\tau$ nor $\beta$ is fixed. ## Conditional dimension bounds

Perfect binary single-error diagnosis with more than two words proves N>=7. Faithful representation of the selected internal algebra C+M3+M3 also proves N>=7. Naming seven roles and the former AP-to-octonions chain do not prove independent axes. See [minimality](/docs/proofs/minimality/theorem-minimality-7).

## Critical Cross-Document Issues

### 1. CS Cascade

**Source:** [Phase Diagram](/docs/core/dynamics/gap-phase-diagram) §1.3 → **Refutation:** [Berry Phase](/docs/physics/cosmology-phys/berry-phase) §2.1

Affected results: $L_\text{top}$, $\beta = 1/(2\pi)$, Noether charges (topological part), equations of motion with topological term, bridge closure via $V_3 \neq 0$.

**Resolution:** Reinterpretation via the [Berry phase](/docs/physics/cosmology-phys/berry-phase). The formula $L_\text{top}$ may be salvaged, but its derivation from CS on 1D is erroneous.

### 2. SM from G₂: rank problem

$\mathrm{rank}(G_2) = 2 < \mathrm{rank}(\text{SM}) = 4$. Electroweak sector: **[C at (FE)]** for the construction, **[H]** for its uniqueness — corrected 2026-09-25; the chain had read [H] → [C at (FE)] → [T] via the uniqueness of the pair $(E,U)$ from $\kappa_0$. The electroweak group acts on the Page–Wootters system factor, not inside $G_2$: in 7D an $SU(2)$ on $\mathrm{span}\{E,U\}$ does not commute with $SU(3)_C$, whose centraliser in $U(7)$ is $U(1)^3$. Correct formulation: '$SU(3)_C$ from $G_2$ [T]; $SU(2)_L \times U(1)_Y$ on the system factor, [C at (FE)]' — [uniqueness theorem](/docs/physics/gauge-symmetry/standard-model#теорема-единственности-фэ).

### 3. CKM predictions: overstatement of precision

The formulae $|V_{us}| \sim \sqrt{m_d/m_s}$ are standard consequences of the Fritzsch texture with observed masses as input. The theory's prediction is the **structure** (Fritzsch texture), not the numbers. **Corrected 2026-09-26 (T-345):** the structure is refuted too — the Fritzsch texture gives $\lvert V_{cb}\rvert\ge0.073$ against $0.0418$ — and no parameter-free structure of the clock predicts a mixing angle or a mass ratio ([CKM §11](/docs/physics/particle-physics/ckm-matrix#11-вкус-с-часов)).

**Empirical status (2026) — retracted 2026-09-26 [✗] (T-345(e)):** the $12.6°$ correction behind $64.5°$ does not exist in the Standard Model (the phase runs by $0.003°$ up to $2\times10^{16}$ GeV), and the uncorrected $77.1°$ is $7.6\sigma$ from $65.7°\pm1.5°$. Former text: The one genuine CKM prediction — the CP phase $\delta_{\text{CP}} \approx 64.5°$ from the Fano geometry [H] — is confirmed near-exactly by the LHCb tree-level combination $\gamma = 64.6° \pm 2.8°$ (ICHEP 2024; $\approx 0.04\sigma$), consistent with the PDG 2024 global fit $65.7° \pm 1.5°$. The older $69° \pm 4°$ figure is superseded across the corpus (SSOT: [CKM §4.2](/docs/physics/particle-physics/ckm-matrix#thm-4-2)).

**Cabibbo Angle Anomaly — resolution-channel predicted [T-265].** First-row CKM unitarity currently shows a $\sim 3.2\sigma$ deficit, $|V_{ud}|^2 + |V_{us}|^2 + |V_{ub}|^2 = 0.9985(5)$ (2024–2026 lattice + $\beta$/kaon determinations). **T-265** ([CKM §10](/docs/physics/particle-physics/ckm-matrix#thm-10-1-t-265)) sharpens the earlier "open gap": since the fundamental CKM would be exactly $3\times3$ unitary, the deficit could not be a mixing-matrix effect — the row claimed fourth generation, vector-like quarks, MeV sterile neutrinos and leptoquarks **all excluded** by the spectrum (corrected 2026-09-25: fourth generation [C at 43c identification], since the count 3 is exact but its physical identification is [I]; vector-like quarks and leptoquarks [H], their grounds — the chirality of $i\Gamma_O\Gamma_A\Gamma_S\Gamma_D$ and T-297 — being retracted or [H]; sterile neutrinos [C]) — so UHM predicts, as a hypothesis, that it resolves in the SM extraction sector ($\gamma W$-box / nuclear radiative corrections / lattice $K/\pi$ / the $K$–$\pi$ $V_{us}$ tension). The resolution **channel** is [H] (corrected from [T-structural]+[C] on 2026-09-25); the **magnitude** of the deficit remains open [D] (see [falsifiability, F-Cabibbo](/docs/reference/falsifiability#f-cabibbo-угол-кабиббо-из-rg-подавления-фано-угла)).

### 4. Sectoral SUSY

The claim '9/21 pairs are exactly compensated' — **refuted** [D]. In standard supergravity SUSY breaks globally. SUSY **does not** contribute new multiplicative suppression to the [Λ budget](/docs/proofs/gap/lambda-budget). See [SUSY from G₂](/docs/physics/particle-physics/susy#susy-lambda).

### 5. Neutrino masses: ratio discrepancy — resolved [C]

~~The naïve seesaw estimate $m_2/m_3 \sim m_\mu^2/m_\tau^2 \sim 0.0035$ disagreed with the observed $m_2/m_3 \sim 0.17$ by ~50×.~~ **Resolved**: O-sector Dirac Yukawa (T-63) reduces the discrepancy from ×50 to ×1.8 (to ×1.2 with the RG correction). Mechanism: $\nu_R$ in the O-sector (T-51) → Dirac mass from blocks $M_{O,3}$ and $M_{O,\bar{3}}$, not from $M_{3,\bar{3}}$. PMNS angles from anarchic $M_R$ — $O(30°\text{–}60°)$ [C]. See [Neutrino Masses](/docs/physics/particle-physics/neutrino-masses#теорема-нейтрино-o-сектор).

## Open mathematical obligations {#эпистемическая-классификация}

Open questions include mathematical hypotheses and bridges, not only computation and data collection. Withdrawn universal closures cannot be used as dependencies. The checkable objects are specified in the [kernel](/docs/reference/mathematical-kernel).

## Dependency structure {#граф-зависимостей}

Selected state object, frame and dynamics → mathematical consequences under explicit hypotheses → reconstruction under an identifiable observation law → physical and phenomenal interpretation under separate bridges. Reverse implications are not assumed. The former automatic AP-to-seven, GKSL-to-three, Gap-to-level and Lawvere-to-no-phenomenal-bridge cascades are withdrawn.

## Rigour Stratification and Framework Dependencies {#стратификация-строгости}

Following the 2026-04-21 proof audit, the theorem stack is stratified by the nature of the rigour supporting each [T] label. This section makes explicit what was previously implicit in individual rows.

### Status tag taxonomy

- **[T]** — theorem with complete rigorous proof: each step either (a) standard mathematical inference, (b) citation to an established result with specific theorem number, or (c) explicit calculation. Mechanisable in a proof assistant (Verum, Lean 4, Coq).
- **[T/sim]** — analytical core is [T]; calibration constants, parameter values, or specific inequalities are cross-checked against SYNARC numerical runs. The simulation is a cross-check, not a replacement for mathematical argument.
- **[T at X]** — rigorous modulo an explicit assumption X (stated in the row).
- **[T mod framework-F]** — legitimately rigorous inside an external framework F (Lurie HTT, Schreiber DCCT, Connes–Chamseddine, Goderis–Verbeure–Vets, Baez–Dolan), where applicability of F to the specific UHM site / construction is either standard or requires separate verification.
- **[C]** — conditional on an explicit hypothesis.
- **[D]** — design choice / definition / convention.
- **[H]** — hypothesis (not yet a theorem).
- **[P]** — postulate.
- **[D]** — definition by convention (e.g. PID as tautological consequence of A1+A2).
- **[I]** — interpretive identification (philosophical mapping between formal structures and phenomenology).
- **[✗]** — retracted.

### Checked kernel and verification scope

Exact identities, finite constructions and counterexamples are listed in the [mathematical kernel](/docs/reference/mathematical-kernel). The former aggregate list of about fifty unconditional theorems is withdrawn; each row has its own scope. A numerical test checks its specified instance and does not replace a universal proof.

### How to read a stratified tag

A tag like **[T at X] + [T/sim] + [D at Y]** means:
- the result is rigorous **given** assumption X (stated explicitly in the row)
- the specific numerical/parameter values are additionally cross-checked against SYNARC simulations
- design choice Y is an engineering specification, not a derivation

This taxonomy does **not** weaken UHM as a theory — it makes the epistemic status of each claim explicit, matching the standard practice of physical theories (general relativity is a theory despite its field equations not being Lean-formalised; Connes–Chamseddine NCG is a theory despite comparable stratification).
